#+TITLE: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS
#+SETUPFILE: ../../clean.theme
#+TODO:
* SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS
:LOCK: T
:END:
private: stuff for implementing ALIENs and friends

- dependencies
  - [[id:COMMON-LISP][COMMON-LISP]]

- dependents
  - [[id:SB-KERNEL][SB-KERNEL]]
  - [[id:SB-ALIEN][SB-ALIEN]]
  - [[id:SB-C][SB-C]]
  - [[id:SB-VM][SB-VM]]

** ALIEN-VALUE-TYPEP :compiled:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-VALUE-TYPEP
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

ALIEN-VALUE-TYPEP names a compiled function:
  Lambda-list: (OBJECT TYPE)
  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T T) *)
  Source file: SYS:SRC;CODE;TARGET-ALIENEVAL.LISP

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;CODE;TARGET-ALIENEVAL.LISP
:end:
** NOTE-LOCAL-ALIEN-TYPE :compiled:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:NOTE-LOCAL-ALIEN-TYPE
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

NOTE-LOCAL-ALIEN-TYPE names a compiled function:
  Lambda-list: (INFO ALIEN)
  Declared type: (FUNCTION (SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:LOCAL-ALIEN-INFO T)
                  (VALUES NULL &OPTIONAL))
  Source file: SYS:SRC;CODE;TARGET-ALIENEVAL.LISP

#+end_example

** ALIEN-FUN-TYPE :class:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-FUN-TYPE
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

ALIEN-FUN-TYPE names the structure-class #<STRUCTURE-CLASS SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-FUN-TYPE>:
  Class precedence-list: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-FUN-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-MEM-BLOCK-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-ALIEN-VALUE-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-SYSTEM-AREA-POINTER-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-TYPE, STRUCTURE-OBJECT,
                         SB-PCL::SLOT-OBJECT, T
  Direct superclasses: SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-MEM-BLOCK-TYPE
  No subclasses.
  Sealed.
  Slots:
    SB-ALIEN::HASH
      Type: (AND FIXNUM UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: 0
    SB-ALIEN::BITS
      Type: (OR NULL UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::ALIGNMENT
      Type: (OR NULL UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::RESULT-TYPE
      Type: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-TYPE
      Initform: (SB-INT:MISSING-ARG)
    SB-ALIEN::ARG-TYPES
      Type: LIST
      Initform: (SB-INT:MISSING-ARG)
    SB-ALIEN::VARARGS
      Type: (OR BOOLEAN FIXNUM (EQL :UNSPECIFIED))
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::STUB
      Type: (OR NULL FUNCTION)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::INTO-STUB
      Type: (OR NULL FUNCTION)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::CONVENTION
      Type: SB-ALIEN::CALLING-CONVENTION
      Initform: NIL

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIENEVAL.LISP
:end:

** ALIEN-VALUE :compiled:function:class:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-VALUE
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

ALIEN-VALUE names a compiled function:
  Lambda-list: (SYMBOL)
  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T) (VALUES T &OPTIONAL))
  Documentation:
    Returns the value of the alien variable bound to SYMBOL. Signals an
    error if SYMBOL is not bound to an alien variable, or if the alien
    variable is undefined.
  Source file: SYS:SRC;CODE;TARGET-ALIENEVAL.LISP

ALIEN-VALUE names the structure-class #<STRUCTURE-CLASS SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-VALUE>:
  Class precedence-list: ALIEN-VALUE, STRUCTURE-OBJECT, SB-PCL::SLOT-OBJECT, T
  Direct superclasses: STRUCTURE-OBJECT
  No subclasses.
  Sealed.
  Slots:
    SB-KERNEL::SAP
      Type: SB-SYS:SYSTEM-AREA-POINTER
      Initform: NIL
    TYPE
      Type: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-TYPE
      Initform: NIL

#+end_example

- SYS:SRC;CODE;TARGET-ALIENEVAL.LISP

- SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIENEVAL.LISP

** OVERRIDE-ALIEN-LINKAGE-ENTRYPOINT :compiled:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:OVERRIDE-ALIEN-LINKAGE-ENTRYPOINT
:ALLOC: HEAP DYNAMIC
:END:
 
#+begin_example

OVERRIDE-ALIEN-LINKAGE-ENTRYPOINT names a compiled function:
  Lambda-list: (C-NAME NEW-VALUE)
  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T T) (VALUES CONS &OPTIONAL))
  Source file: SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIEN-CALLBACK.LISP

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIEN-CALLBACK.LISP
:end:
** ALIEN-RECORD-TYPE :class:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-RECORD-TYPE
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

ALIEN-RECORD-TYPE names the structure-class #<STRUCTURE-CLASS SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-RECORD-TYPE>:
  Class precedence-list: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-RECORD-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-MEM-BLOCK-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-ALIEN-VALUE-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-SYSTEM-AREA-POINTER-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-TYPE, STRUCTURE-OBJECT,
                         SB-PCL::SLOT-OBJECT, T
  Direct superclasses: SB-ALIEN::ALIEN-MEM-BLOCK-TYPE
  No subclasses.
  Sealed.
  Slots:
    SB-ALIEN::HASH
      Type: (AND FIXNUM UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: 0
    SB-ALIEN::BITS
      Type: (OR NULL UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::ALIGNMENT
      Type: (OR NULL UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::KIND
      Type: (MEMBER :STRUCT :UNION)
      Initform: :STRUCT
    SB-ALIEN::NAME
      Type: (OR SYMBOL NULL)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::FIELDS
      Type: LIST
      Initform: NIL

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIENEVAL.LISP
:end:

** ALIEN-SUBTYPE-P :compiled:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-SUBTYPE-P
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

ALIEN-SUBTYPE-P names a compiled function:
  Lambda-list: (TYPE1 TYPE2)
  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T T) *)
  Documentation:
    Return T iff the alien type TYPE1 is a subtype of TYPE2. Currently, the
    only supported subtype relationships are is that any pointer type is a
    subtype of (* t), and any array type first dimension will match
    (array <eltype> nil ...). Otherwise, the two types have to be
    ALIEN-TYPE-=.
  Source file: SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIENEVAL.LISP

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIENEVAL.LISP
:end:

:PROPERTIES:

#+begin_example

ALIEN-ENUM-TYPE names the structure-class #<STRUCTURE-CLASS SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-ENUM-TYPE>:
  Class precedence-list: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-ENUM-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-INTEGER-TYPE,
                         SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-TYPE, STRUCTURE-OBJECT,
                         SB-PCL::SLOT-OBJECT, T
  Direct superclasses: SB-ALIEN-INTERNALS:ALIEN-INTEGER-TYPE
  No subclasses.
  Sealed.
  Slots:
    SB-ALIEN::HASH
      Type: (AND FIXNUM UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: 0
    SB-ALIEN::BITS
      Type: (OR NULL UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::ALIGNMENT
      Type: (OR NULL UNSIGNED-BYTE)
      Initform: NIL
    SIGNED
      Type: (MEMBER T NIL)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::NAME
      Type: T
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::FROM
      Type: T
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::TO
      Type: T
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::KIND
      Type: T
      Initform: NIL
    SB-ALIEN::OFFSET
      Type: T
      Initform: NIL

#+end_example

- SYS:SRC;CODE;ALIENEVAL.LISP
:end:
